### [129] [A Counterexample to Wegner's Conjecture for Axis-Parallel Rectangles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17854)
*Deepak Ajwani,Rishikesh Gajjala,Rajiv Raman,Saurabh Ray*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: Wegner (1965)猜想每个平面轴平行矩形族满足τ(R) ≤ 2ν(R)-1，本文构造反例证明该猜想不成立，并给出τ(R) ≥ 2.21ν(R)的更一般构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wegner猜想自1965年提出以来持续推动了大量研究，但始终未被证明或否定。本文旨在通过构造反例最终解决这一长期悬而未决的问题。

Method: 构造一个三角形自由的矩形交图，其顶点独立数≤ n/4。由于三角形自由，每个穿刺点至多覆盖两个矩形，从而τ(R) ≥ n/2 ≥ 2ν(R)，推翻Wegner猜想。进一步推广构造得到τ(R) ≥ 2.21ν(R)。

Result: 明确构造了Wegner猜想的反例，并建立了更强的下界τ(R) ≥ 2.21ν(R)，表明最大独立矩形集问题的标准点松弛（即团松弛）的积分间隙至少为2.21。

Conclusion: Wegner猜想不成立，τ(R)与ν(R)之间的实际比值可以大于2，这为穿刺问题的近似算法和松弛方法提供了新的下界约束。

Abstract: In 1965, Wegner conjectured that every finite family \(\mathcal R\) of axis-parallel rectangles in the plane satisfies \(τ(\mathcal R) \le 2ν(\mathcal R)-1\), where \(τ(\mathcal R)\) denotes the minimum number of points needed to pierce all rectangles in \(\mathcal R\), and \(ν(\mathcal R)\) denotes the maximum size of a pairwise disjoint subfamily. Over the last six decades, the conjecture has motivated a long line of work: it has been verified for several special classes of rectangle families, and the best known general upper bounds have been progressively improved, but the conjecture itself had remained open. We give an explicit counterexample.
  More precisely, we construct a triangle-free rectangle-intersection graph on \(n\) vertices whose independence number is at most \(n/4\). Since the graph is triangle-free, no point of the plane can lie in three rectangles; hence every piercing point hits at most two rectangles. Consequently, \(τ(\mathcal R) \ge n/2 \ge 2ν(\mathcal R)\), contradicting Wegner's conjectured bound. We also give a slightly more general construction for which \(τ(\mathcal R) \ge 2.21ν(\mathcal R)\). This shows that the standard point relaxation, equivalently the clique relaxation, for the Maximum Independent Set of Rectangles problem has integrality gap at least \(2.21\).

</details>


### [130] [Greedy Vector Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17991)
*Wojciech Czerwiński,Daniel Dadush,Ekin Ergen,Arka Ghosh,Sławomir Lasota,Łukasz Orlikowski*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 本文分析了欧几里得贪心向量平衡算法，证明了当向量集合T有限时，该算法产生的带符号前缀和的欧几里得范数存在一个不依赖于序列长度n的有限上界(2/δ_T)^{d-1}，其中δ_T是T中向量与其张成子空间的最小非零距离。该结果还被推广到在线向量划分问题，并应用于证明固定场景数下字典序总完工时间调度问题的多项式时间可解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线向量平衡问题中，向量序列依次到达，需要在线分配符号以最小化带符号前缀和的最大范数。本文旨在分析自然贪心策略（每一步选择使当前向量与历史累加和内积非正的符号）的性能，并给出与序列长度n无关的有限界。

Method: 通过构造一个T-吸收的有界凸集K_T（满足若x∈K_T且⟨x,t⟩≤0则x+t∈K_T），并显式构造包含在半径(2/δ_T)^{d-1}球内的K_T，其构造基于T中向量张成子空间的链。同时给出了下界Ω(√d/δ_T)的简单集合构造。

Result: 1) 对于由单位向量组成的有限集T，贪心算法产生的带符号前缀和范数不超过(2/δ_T)^{d-1}；2) 该上界在向量缩放的序列上同样成立；3) 存在集合T使得下界为Ω(√d/δ_T)；4) 将结果推广到在线向量划分问题；5) 作为应用，证明了Bosman等人猜想的一个特例。

Conclusion: 首次给出了有限向量集上贪心向量平衡算法的不依赖于序列长度的有限性能界，通过构造T-吸收集K_T的方法为分析此类在线算法提供了新工具。该结果在在线向量划分和调度问题中具有应用价值。

Abstract: In online vector balancing, vectors $t_1,\dots,t_n$ arrive one by one from a given set $T$ and the goal is to assign signs $s_1,\dots,s_n\in\{\pm1\}$ in an online manner so as to minimize the largest norm of any signed prefix sum $\sum_{i=1}^ks_i t_i$, $k \in [n]$. In this paper, we analyze the natural Euclidean greedy vector balancing algorithm for this problem: at each step $k$, the sign $s_k\in\{\pm1\}$ is chosen so that $s_k t_k$ has non-positive inner product with $\sum_{i=1}^{k-1} s_i\cdot t_i$. Our main result is the first finite bound, independent of the sequence length $n$, on the performance of greedy whenever $T$ is finite. When $T \subset \mathbb{R}^d$ consists of unit vectors, we prove that the signed sums produced by greedy have Euclidean norm at most $(2/δ_T)^{d-1}$, where $δ_T$ is the minimum non-zero distance between vectors in $T$ and subspaces spanned by vectors in $T$. The same upper bound holds when the sequences are composed of scaled down vectors in $T$. We also provide a simple set $T$ for which $Ω(\sqrt{d}/δ_T)$ is a lower bound.
  We analyze the greedy algorithm by proving the existence of a bounded convex $K_T$ that is $T$-absorbing: $\forall x\in K_T$ and $t \in\pm T$, $\langle x,t\rangle\leq0\Rightarrow x+t\in K_T$. We give an explicit construction of a set $K_T$ contained in a ball of radius $(2/δ_T)^{d-1}$, based on chains of subspaces spanned by vectors in $T$, which may be of independent interest.
  We generalize our greedy vector balancing bound to online vector partitioning, where the sequence $t_1,\dots,t_n$ must be partitioned in an online manner into $p$ subsequences. As an application, we prove a special case of a conjecture of Bosman et al. (arxiv:2402.19259), showing that a lexicographic version of total completion time scheduling under scenarios is polynomial time solvable when the number of scenarios is fixed.

</details>

### [131] [Improved limits on a new $Z'$ in $B-L$ scenarios with the NA64 experiment at CERN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17320)
*The NA64 Collaboration,Yu. M. Andreev,A. Antonov,M. A. Ayala Torres,D. Banerjee,B. Banto Oberhauser,V. Bautin,J. Bernhard,P. Bisio,A. Celentano,N. Charitonidis,P. Crivelli,A. V. Dermenev,S. V. Donskov,R. R. Dusaev,T. Enik,V. N. Frolov,S. V. Gertsenberger,S. Girod,S. N. Gninenko,M. Hösgen,Y. Kambar,A. E. Karneyeu,G. Kekelidze,B. Ketzer,D. V. Kirpichnikov,M. M. Kirsanov,V. A. Kramarenko,L. V. Kravchuk,N. V. Krasnikov,S. V. Kuleshov,V. E. Lyubovitskij,V. Lysan,A. Marini,L. Marsicano,V. A. Matveev,R. Mena Fredes,R. Mena Yanssen,L. Molina Bueno,M. Mongillo,D. V. Peshekhonov,V. A. Polyakov,B. Radics,K. Salamatin,V. D. Samoylenko,H. Sieber,D. Shchukin,O. Soto,V. O. Tikhomirov,I. Tlisova,A. N. Toropin,M. Tuzi,P. V. Volkov,I. V. Voronchikhin,J. Zamora-Sa'a,A. S. Zhevlakov*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: NA64实验利用2016-2022年收集的完整电子束数据（$9.4\times10^{11}$电子打靶），对$U(1)_{B-L}$规范玻色子$Z'$的耦合常数$g_{B-L}$给出了新的约束。在$m_{Z'}\in[200,300]$ MeV质量范围内，共振$e^{+}e^{-}$湮灭产生通道增强了灵敏度。对于未破缺的$U(1)_{B-L}$情形，新限制超越了专用中微子散射实验，为亚GeV质量的新玻色子提供了最严格的实验室界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型的扩展模型中，引入额外的$U(1)_{B-L}$规范对称性可将中微子质量的起源与暗物质候选者联系起来。相关的$Z'$规范玻色子直接耦合到标准模型费米子，可通过电子-核相互作用在固定靶实验中产生，因此有必要通过实验对其耦合参数进行约束。

Method: 利用NA64实验2016-2022年收集的完整电子束数据集（$9.4\times10^{11}$电子打靶），通过分析共振$e^{+}e^{-}$湮灭产生通道来约束$g_{B-L}$，该通道在$m_{Z'}\in[200,300]$ MeV质量范围内增强了灵敏度。对于$Z'$耦合到暗物质的场景，从NA64不可见模式分析直接推导排除限。

Result: 新数据集统计量约为此前分析的三倍，提升了灵敏度。对于未破缺$U(1)_{B-L}$情形，新限制超越了专用中微子散射实验，为亚GeV质量的新玻色子提供了最严格的实验室界限。

Conclusion: NA64实验利用更大统计量的电子束数据，对$U(1)_{B-L}$模型中的$Z'$玻色子耦合常数给出了目前最严格的实验室约束，尤其在亚GeV质量区间超越了专用中微子散射实验的界限。

Abstract: Extensions of the Standard Model featuring an additional $U(1)_{B-L}$ gauge symmetry provide a compelling framework linking the origin of neutrino masses to possible dark matter candidates. The associated gauge boson, $Z'$, couples directly to Standard Model fermions and can be produced in fixed-target experiments through electron-nucleus interactions. In this work, we present new constraints on the coupling constant $g_{B-L}$ obtained with the NA64 experiment using the full electron-beam dataset collected between 2016 and 2022, corresponding to $(9.4\pm0.5)\times10^{11}$ electrons on target. The analysis includes the resonant $e^{+}e^{-}$ annihilation production channel, which enhances sensitivity in the mass range $m_{Z'}\in[200,300]$ MeV. The larger dataset provides approximately three times the statistics of previous analyses, thereby improving sensitivity. For the unbroken $U(1)_{B-L}$ case, the new limits exceed those from dedicated neutrino-scattering experiments, providing the most stringent laboratory bounds on $g_{B-L}$ for sub-GeV masses of the new boson. In scenarios where the $Z'$ couples to dark matter, the decay width is dominated by invisible channels, and the corresponding exclusion limits can be directly derived from the NA64 invisible-mode analysis.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文报告了费米国家加速器实验室（FNAL）Muon g-2实验利用2021-2023年收集的正缪子数据对缪子磁异常a_μ的最终测量结果。新数据集统计量是先前结果的2.5倍以上，得到a_μ=116 592 0710(162)×10^{-12}（139 ppb），与之前结果结合后为116 592 0705(148)×10^{-12}（127 ppb）。新的实验世界平均值为a_μ^{Exp}=116 592 0715(145)×10^{-12}（124 ppb），以FNAL测量为主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对缪子磁异常a_μ进行更高精度的测量，以检验标准模型的理论预测与实验测量之间是否存在差异，从而探索超出标准模型的新物理。本工作旨在利用更大的统计样本进一步提高测量精度。

Method: 通过测量缪子存储环磁场中反常自旋进动频率与屏蔽质子进动频率的比值，结合外部已知常数（22 ppb精度水平），从2021-2023年收集的正缪子数据中确定a_μ的值。

Result: 新数据集得到a_μ=116 592 0710(162)×10^{-12}（139 ppb精度）；与先前结果结合后为116 592 0705(148)×10^{-12}（127 ppb精度）；新的实验世界平均值为a_μ^{Exp}=116 592 0715(145)×10^{-12}（124 ppb精度）。

Conclusion: FNAL Muon g-2实验完成了对缪子磁异常a_μ的最终测量，新结果精度达到139 ppb，结合先前数据后提升至127 ppb，新的实验世界平均值以FNAL测量结果为主导，为检验标准模型理论预测提供了关键实验数据。

Abstract: This report details the final measurement of the muon magnetic anomaly, $a_μ=(g_μ-2)/2$, by the Muon $g-2$ experiment at Fermi National Accelerator Laboratory (FNAL), using positive muons collected from 2021 to 2023. The value of $a_μ$ is determined from the ratio of the anomalous spin precession frequency to the shielded proton precession frequency in the muon storage ring magnetic field, combined with external constants known at the 22 ppb level. The new dataset, containing over $2.5$ times the statistics of our previous results, yields $a_μ=116\,592\,0710(162)\times 10^{-12}$ (139 ppb), or $a_μ=116\,592\,0705(148)\times 10^{-12}$ (127 ppb) when combined with our previous results. The new experimental world average, dominated by the measurements at FNAL, is $a_μ^{\text{Exp}}=116\,592\,0715(145)\times 10^{-12}$ (124 ppb).

</details>


### [133] [Better Queries, Cheaper Attention: Adapting Transformers for Efficient Sparse Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17631)
*Philippa Duckett,Samuel Van Stroud,Max Hart,Gabriel Facini,Tim Scanlon*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何感知动态查询解码器（DQ+LSCA），用于从稀疏科学传感器测量中高效重建物体（如带电粒子轨迹）。该方法通过输入条件化的查询初始化和局部跨步交叉注意力机制，在简化的大型强子对撞机探测器模拟中，将轨迹重建效率从94.1%提升至98.1%，虚警率降低超过一半，推理延迟降低近50%，峰值内存占用降低超过10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于查询的Transformer解码器在从稀疏传感器测量中重建物体时表现有效，但存在两个关键局限：固定且与输入无关的查询集限制了向高多重性数据的可扩展性，以及解码器交叉注意力计算成本高昂。

Method: 提出几何感知动态查询解码器，包含两个核心设计：(1) 输入条件化查询构建——从编码器级的测量表示中选择候选轨迹种子来初始化解码器查询，使查询内容和数量都依赖于输入；(2) 局部跨步交叉注意力（LSCA）——利用诱导出的几何排序，用几何定义的局部支持区域替代学习的掩码门控交叉注意力，将注意力限制在物理上合理的查询-击中交互上，并暴露稀疏性以支持块稀疏执行。

Result: 在简化的大型强子对撞机探测器模拟中，动态查询架构将轨迹重建效率从94.1%提升至98.1%，虚警率降低超过一半。DQ+LSCA模型相比固定查询基线，端到端推理延迟降低近50%，峰值推理内存分配降低超过10倍。

Conclusion: 几何感知动态查询解码器有效克服了固定查询Transformer在稀疏传感器数据重建中的可扩展性和计算效率问题，为高多重性科学数据重建提供了一种高效且准确的解决方案。

Abstract: Query-based transformer decoders are effective for object reconstruction from sparse scientific sensor measurements, but their scalability to high-multiplicity data is limited by fixed, input-independent query sets and costly decoder cross-attention. We introduce a geometry-aware dynamic-query decoder that couples input-conditioned query construction with structured sparse cross-attention. Decoder queries are initialised from selected encoder-level measurement representations that serve as candidate trajectory seeds, making both query content and query multiplicity input-dependent. Local Strided Cross-Attention (LSCA) exploits the induced geometric ordering by replacing learned mask-gated cross-attention with a geometry-defined local support that restricts attention to physically plausible query-hit interactions and exposes sparsity for block-sparse execution. We study this architecture for charged-particle trajectory reconstruction in a simplified High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider detector, where thousands of trajectories must be reconstructed from tens of thousands of sparse measurements. In the nominal configuration, the dynamic-query (DQ) architecture increases trajectory reconstruction efficiency from 94.1% to 98.1% and reduces the fake rate by more than a factor of two relative to the fixed-query baseline. The DQ+LSCA model reduces end-to-end inference latency by nearly 50% and peak allocated inference memory by more than a factor of 10 relative to the fixed-query baseline.

</details>

Zhemchugov,B. Zheng,B. M. Zheng,J. P. Zheng,W. J. Zheng,X. R. Zheng,Y. H. Zheng,B. Zhong,C. Zhong,H. Zhou,J. Q. Zhou,J. Y. Zhou,S. Zhou,X. Zhou,X. K. Zhou,X. R. Zhou,X. Y. Zhou,Y. X. Zhou,Y. Z. Zhou,A. N. Zhu,J. Zhu,K. Zhu,K. J. Zhu,K. S. Zhu,L. Zhu,L. X. Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用BESIII探测器在质心能量3.773 GeV处收集的20.3 fb⁻¹数据，首次对D⁺→π⁺ηη衰变进行振幅分析。唯一显著成分是中间过程D⁺→a₀(980)⁺η, a₀(980)⁺→π⁺η，其分支比为(3.67±0.12_stat±0.06_syst)×10⁻³。观测到的π⁺η质量谱线形与D(s)→a₀(980)π及D⁰→a₀(980)⁻e⁺νₑ衰变中的显著不同。多种常规a₀(980)振幅描述（Flatté、色散修正Flatté、T-矩阵、K-矩阵）及其扩展均不能令人满意地再现观测线形。允许参数浮动时可获得满意拟合，但极点质量被推至KK阈值以上，与a₀(980)的近阈值特性不一致。结果揭示了传统直接产生振幅模型中拟合质量与物理极点位置之间的张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对D⁺→π⁺ηη衰变进行首次振幅分析，研究a₀(980)介子的性质，特别是检验不同振幅模型描述该衰变过程中产生的a₀(980)线形能力。

Method: 使用BESIII探测器收集的20.3 fb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻对撞数据，在√s=3.773 GeV能量下进行振幅分析。采用多种参数化模型（Flatté、色散修正Flatté、T-矩阵、K-矩阵）描述a₀(980)振幅，并比较固定与浮动参数下的拟合效果。

Result: 唯一显著成分是D⁺→a₀(980)⁺η, a₀(980)⁺→π⁺η，分支比(3.67±0.12±0.06)×10⁻³。观测到的π⁺η质量谱线形与D(s)→a₀(980)π和D⁰→a₀(980)⁻e⁺νₑ中的不同。常规模型及扩展均不能满意拟合观测线形；允许参数浮动时可拟合，但极点质量高于KK阈值，与a₀(980)物理特性矛盾。

Conclusion: D⁺→π⁺ηη衰变中a₀(980)的线形与其它产生过程存在显著差异，传统直接产生振幅模型在拟合质量与物理极点位置之间存在根本性张力，表明对a₀(980)的理解可能需要超越当前模型的更深入理论框架。

Abstract: Using $20.3~{\rm fb}^{-1}$ of $e^+e^-$ collision data collected with the BESIII detector at $\sqrt{s}=3.773~{\rm GeV}$, we perform the first amplitude analysis of the decay $D^+\toπ^+ηη$. The intermediate process $D^+\to a_0(980)^+η$, $a_0(980)^+\toπ^+η$, is observed as the only significant component in the amplitude analysis, and its branching fraction is measured to be $(3.67\pm0.12_{\rm stat}\pm0.06_{\rm syst})\times10^{-3}$. The $π^+η$ mass spectrum associated with $a_0(980)^+η$ production exhibits a line shape that differs substantially from those observed in $D_{(s)}\to a_0(980)π$ and $D^0\to a_0(980)^-e^+ν_e$ decays. We examine several conventional descriptions of the $a_0(980)$ amplitude, including Flatté, dispersively modified Flatté, $T$-matrix, and $K$-matrix parameterizations. With reference $a_0(980)$ parameters, neither these models nor their extensions including additional small resonant or non-resonant amplitudes reproduce the observed line shape satisfactorily. When the $a_0(980)$ parameters are allowed to float, satisfactory fits can be obtained, but the pole mass is driven well above the $K\bar K$ threshold, inconsistent with the near-threshold character of the $a_0(980)$. The results reveal a tension between fit quality and the physical pole position in conventional direct-production amplitude models.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [On the Strong Duality in Continuous-time and Discrete-time Linear Quadratic Regulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17208)
*Yuto Watanabe,Yang Zheng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了连续时间和离散时间系统线性二次型调节器（LQR）中的强对偶性，通过线性算子Ψ构建统一的非凸LQR问题，推导拉格朗日对偶问题，并利用秩约束半定规划（SDP）松弛建立强对偶性，最终证明SDP松弛的最优解可构造性地恢复经典代数Riccati方程和最优反馈增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视LQR问题中的强对偶性，探索其与典型假设及原始-对偶解唯一性之间的内在联系。

Method: 1) 利用线性算子Ψ构建统一的非凸LQR问题框架，涵盖连续时间和离散时间系统；2) 推导拉格朗日对偶问题；3) 通过秩约束的紧半定规划（SDP）松弛建立强对偶性；4) 利用线性算子及其伴随算子的性质揭示两个时间域之间的结构对称性。

Result: SDP松弛（去除秩约束后）的原始-对偶最优解能以构造性方式恢复经典代数Riccati方程和最优反馈增益。

Conclusion: 强对偶性的建立在温和的标准假设下即可成立，且本文揭示了两类时间域（连续与离散）在算子层面的结构对称性。

Abstract: This paper revisits the strong duality in the linear quadratic regulator (LQR) for continuous-time and discrete-time systems, and explores its interconnection with typical assumptions and the uniqueness of primal-dual solutions. Using a linear operator $Ψ$, we formulate a common nonconvex LQR problem that captures both time domains. We then derive its Lagrange dual problem and establish the strong duality via a rank-constrained tight semidefinite program (SDP) relaxation. Further, we show that the primal-dual optimal solutions to the SDP relaxation, after dropping the rank constraint, recover the classical algebraic Riccati equations and optimal feedback gains in a constructive manner. The dual derivation and strong duality analysis rely on mild standard assumptions and exploit the properties of the linear operator and its adjoint, revealing a structural symmetry between the two time domains.

</details>


### [136] [Accelerated Convex Optimization via Hamiltonian Dynamics with Deterministic Integration Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17260)
*Xiuyuan Wang,Vishwak Srinivasan,Qiang Fu,Siddharth Mitra,Ashia Wilson,Andre Wibisono*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出基于哈密顿动力学的确定性加速凸优化算法，通过利用平均哈密顿流轨迹的收缩性（而非终点收缩），实现了无需随机性或目标为二次型假设的加速收敛保证，并给出了具有最优一阶复杂度的离散实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有哈密顿动力学优化方法局限于二次目标函数或仅在期望意义下成立（随机性），缺乏对一般平滑凸优化的确定性加速收敛保证。本文旨在填补这一空白，建立哈密顿动力学作为确定性加速凸优化通用工具的理论基础。

Method: 1) 分析理想连续时间哈密顿动力学算法的收敛性，利用平均轨迹收缩性而非端点收缩性；2) 推导具有最优一阶复杂度的实用离散时间实现算法。

Result: 1) 证明了哈密顿动力学方法在一般平滑凸优化上具有确定性的加速收敛速率；2) 离散实现达到了最优一阶复杂度（即信息论下界）。

Conclusion: 哈密顿动力学可以作为确定性加速凸优化的一个有效算法基元，扩展了其理论适用范围，并提供了可实际部署的高效算法。

Abstract: We develop Hamiltonian dynamics-based algorithms for smooth convex optimization that achieve accelerated rates of convergence. By exploiting contraction of averaged Hamiltonian flow trajectories rather than requiring contraction at trajectory endpoints, we show that Hamiltonian dynamics-based optimization methods admit deterministic and accelerated convergence guarantees, extending prior work that is limited to quadratic objectives or holds only in expectation. We analyze an idealized continuous-time algorithm and derive practical discrete-time implementations with optimal first-order complexity, thereby establishing Hamiltonian dynamics as a useful algorithmic primitive for deterministic accelerated convex optimization.

</details>


### [137] [Adaptive Proximal Methods for Weakly Convex Optimization with Unknown Parameter: Deterministic and Stochastic Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17285)
*Miaolan Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应邻近引导方案（APS），一种无需已知弱凸参数ρ的单次尝试邻近算法，用于最小化ρ-弱凸非光滑非凸目标函数。该方法通过下降测试在线双向调整邻近参数，在确定性和随机设定下均能达到O(ε⁻²)迭代复杂度，且随机设定中允许有偏、重尾的函数差估计和低精度随机邻近算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱凸优化方法通常需要已知弱凸参数ρ，但在许多实际学习与信号恢复问题中ρ未知。本文旨在设计一种无需ρ先验知识、能自动适应局部结构且鲁棒性强的邻近算法。

Method: 提出APS算法：通过双向下降测试在线自适应调整邻近参数，无需全局Lipschitz连续性或光滑性假设。在确定性设定中利用下降测试确保进展；在随机设定中采用高概率分析框架，仅需常概率精度的随机邻近算子，容忍有偏和重尾噪声。

Result: 确定性设定：达到O(ε⁻²)迭代复杂度获取ε-次梯度稳定点。随机设定：以高概率达到O(ε⁻²)迭代界使Moreau包络梯度低于ε，且对随机邻近算子的精度要求极弱（仅需在参数低于1/(2ρ)时以常概率足够精确）。

Conclusion: APS算法首次在ρ未知条件下实现弱凸优化的高效自适应求解，理论保证扎实且对噪声假设极为宽松，具有实用价值。

Abstract: Many nonsmooth, nonconvex objectives in learning and signal recovery are $ρ$-weakly convex. We minimize such a function in deterministic and stochastic settings when the weak-convexity parameter $ρ$ is unknown. The objective is not required to be globally Lipschitz continuous or smooth. We propose the Adaptive Prox-Guided Scheme (APS), a one-trial proximal algorithm that adapts the proximal parameter online and bidirectionally through a descent test, allowing it to exploit favorable local structure.
  In the deterministic setting, APS obtains an $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ iteration complexity for producing an $\varepsilon$-subgradient stationary point. In the stochastic setting, APS achieves a high-probability $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ iteration bound for driving the Moreau-envelope gradient below $\varepsilon$. This result holds under deliberately weak oracle assumptions: the function-difference estimates may be biased and heavy-tailed, and the stochastic proximal oracle need only be sufficiently accurate with constant probability when the proximal parameter lies below $1/(2ρ)$ (unknown to the algorithm), and can be arbitrary otherwise.

</details>


### [138] [Coarse Preference Reporting in the Bottleneck Model: Approximate Strategyproofness and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17400)
*Takara Sakai,Riki Kawase*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究基于粗粒度时间槽的瓶颈动态系统最优（DSO）机制，车辆从有限时间槽菜单中选择报告，运营商据此分配通行时间并征收容量影子价格作为通行费。理论证明最坏情况谎报收益和期望效率损失均随槽宽二次衰减，且通行费具有激励真实报告的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VCG机制虽然能实现策略证明性，但规则复杂且计算负担大。实践中预约和分配系统（如自动车道管理、机场时隙分配、配送预约窗口）均采用离散时间槽的粗粒度报告接口。本文旨在设计一种基于时间槽的DSO机制，在粗粒度报告下实现近似的系统最优和社会效率。

Method: 设计基于时间槽的DSO机制：车辆从有限时间槽菜单中选择报告，运营商根据报告槽实现DSO分配，并征收容量影子价格作为通行费。理论上分析最坏情况谎报收益和期望效率损失随槽宽的衰减率，并与无通行费情况对比。通过数值实验验证理论结果在充分条件之外参数区域的鲁棒性。

Result: 最坏情况谎报收益和期望效率损失均随槽宽二次衰减；效率损失在容量约束下呈二次衰减，最坏情况谎报收益需额外条件（偏好到达时间分布和调度成本函数）。无通行费时，即使槽无限细化，谎报激励仍然存在，说明通行费具有激励真实报告的关键作用。

Conclusion: 基于粗粒度时间槽的DSO机制在理论上有良好的效率保证（二次衰减），数值实验支持其在充分条件外的鲁棒性。通行费不仅是容量定价工具，更是激励真实报告的关键机制。

Abstract: A central operator schedules each vehicle's passage time through a bottleneck to achieve a dynamic system optimum (DSO). The assignment depends on each vehicle's preferred arrival time, which is private and must be elicited from each vehicle. Mechanisms that elicit exact preferences, such as the Vickrey-Clarke-Groves (VCG) mechanism, can achieve strategyproofness but involve relatively complex rules and a computational burden on the operator. We focus instead on coarse reporting, in which each vehicle selects from a finite menu of time slots of a common width. This discrete interface already structures reservation and appointment systems in practice, including managed lanes for automated vehicles, airport slot allocation, and delivery appointment windows. We design a slot-based DSO mechanism on this coarse interface, in which the operator implements DSO assignment based on the reported slots and charges a capacity shadow price as a toll, and evaluate its performance. We prove that both the worst-case misreporting gain and the expected efficiency loss decrease quadratically in the slot width. The efficiency loss decays in this way under binding capacity, while the worst-case misreporting gain requires an additional condition on the preferred arrival time distribution and the schedule cost function. Analyzing the no-toll case, we find that the misreporting incentive persists, however finely the slots are refined, indicating that the toll also serves to elicit truthful reports. Numerical experiments support these theoretical results and show that they continue to hold in parameter regions outside the sufficient conditions.

</details>


### [139] [Contextual Robust Optimization for AI Data Center Scheduling with Statistical Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17466)
*Yijie Yang,Xi Weng,Yue Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向AI数据中心运行的上下文鲁棒优化框架，通过损失驱动的不确定性学习模型捕捉可再生能源和AI工作负载的预测误差，并构建可解的联合机会约束调度问题，在真实数据上实现平均5.57%的成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI工作负载快速增长导致数据中心电力需求和碳排放显著增加，而现有碳感知调度方法因可再生能源发电和AI工作负载的预测误差以及训练/推理工作负载的异构计算特性而效果有限。

Method: 提出上下文鲁棒优化框架：1) 显式建模AI训练与推理的异构计算特性；2) 构建损失驱动的不确定性学习模型，将上下文特征直接映射为协变量相关的不确定性集；3) 将联合机会约束调度问题重构为可解鲁棒优化问题；4) 设计校准算法提供有限样本概率可行性保证。

Result: 基于真实AI工作负载轨迹和可再生能源数据的数值实验表明，相比基准方法，所提方法平均降低运营成本5.57%，同时保持可靠的可行性和较强的计算可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提出的上下文鲁棒优化框架能有效应对预测误差和异构工作负载挑战，在降低AI数据中心运营成本的同时保证调度方案的可行性与可扩展性。

Abstract: The rapid growth of AI workloads is substantially increasing data center electricity demand and carbon emissions, motivating the development of carbon-aware scheduling methods. However, effective scheduling is challenging because renewable generation and AI workloads are subject to forecast errors, while training and inference workloads exhibit heterogeneity in computational characteristics. This paper proposes a contextual robust optimization framework for AI data center operation. The proposed model explicitly captures the heterogeneous computational characteristics of AI training and inference workloads. To deal with renewable generation and workload forecast errors, we develop loss-based uncertainty learning models that directly map contextual features to covariate-dependent uncertainty sets. The resulting contextual joint chance-constrained scheduling problem is reformulated into a tractable robust optimization problem, and a calibration algorithm is developed to provide finite-sample probabilistic feasibility guarantees for multiple joint chance constraints. Numerical experiments based on real-world AI workload traces and renewable generation data show that the proposed method reduces operating costs by an average of 5.57% compared to benchmark methods while maintaining reliable feasibility and strong computational scalability.

</details>


### [140] [Beyond IGO-Flow: Toward Convergence Analysis of IGO in Continuous Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17523)
*Ryosuke Kimura,Youhei Akimoto*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了离散时间信息几何优化（IGO）框架在高斯族上的收敛性，证明在强凸二次目标函数下，协方差矩阵收敛到零矩阵，且在合适条件下均值向量收敛到全局最优解，弥合了IGO理论与CMA-ES等实际算法之间的理论差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IGO为黑箱优化提供了统一框架，但其收敛理论主要局限于连续时间理想化模型（如IGO流），缺乏对离散时间更新（非无穷小学习率）的理论分析，亟需弥合理论与实际算法（如CMA-ES）之间的鸿沟。

Method: 在指数族期望参数坐标下将离散时间IGO表述为自然梯度更新，选取多元高斯分布族作为搜索分布，针对强凸二次目标函数进行分析，同时考虑全协方差自适应、固定正学习率和基于分位数的权重。

Result: 证明了协方差矩阵收敛到零矩阵；进一步证明，若适当缩放后的协方差矩阵的条件数在足够频繁的迭代中有界，则均值向量收敛到全局最优解。

Conclusion: 推进了IGO的离散时间收敛理论，建立了IGO理论与实际协方差自适应搜索方法（如CMA-ES）之间的理论联系。

Abstract: Information-Geometric Optimization (IGO) provides a unified framework for black-box optimization by interpreting the adaptation of a search distribution as a natural gradient update. Despite its conceptual importance, the convergence theory of IGO remains limited: most existing results concern continuous-time idealizations such as the IGO flow, rather than discrete-time updates with non-infinitesimal learning rates. In this paper, we study discrete-time IGO in continuous spaces, formulated as natural gradient updates in the expectation-parameter coordinates of an exponential family. In particular, we analyze IGO over the multivariate Gaussian family on strongly convex quadratic objective functions. Our analysis covers a setting that simultaneously incorporates full covariance adaptation, a fixed positive learning rate, and quantile-based weights. In this setting, we prove that the covariance matrix converges to the zero matrix. We further show that the mean vector converges to the global optimum, provided that the condition number of the appropriately scaled covariance matrix is bounded at sufficiently frequent iterations. These results advance the convergence theory of IGO and help bridge the gap between the mathematical theory of IGO and practical covariance-adaptive search methods such as CMA-ES.

</details>


### [141] [An Adaptive Algorithm for the Approximation of General Linear-Parametric Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17552)
*Levin Nemesch,Stefan Ruzika,Clemens Thielen,Alina Wittmann*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的自适应近似（及精确）算法，适用于更广泛类别的线性多参数优化问题，克服了现有参数近似算法不允许负参数依赖且参数集固定为正象限的限制，并证明了线性多参数最大化问题中非负最优目标值假设不足以确保可近似性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数近似算法存在共同假设限制（不允许负参数依赖、参数集固定为正象限），限制了可近似参数优化问题的类别，需要更通用的算法。

Method: 提出一种新的自适应近似（及精确）算法，基于参数优化和多目标优化领域的现有算法进行推广，并提供参数集变换的结构性结果。

Result: 新算法适用于更广泛的线性多参数优化问题；对于线性多参数最大化问题，非负最优目标值假设不足以确保可近似性。

Conclusion: 新算法扩展了可处理的参数优化问题范围，揭示了现有近似性条件（非负最优目标值）的局限性，为后续研究提供了方向。

Abstract: Linear-multi-parametric optimization problems are a widely studied class of optimization problems. The objective function in such a problem is affine linear dependent on a parameter vector, and the goal is to compute a set of solutions that contains an optimal solution for every fixed parameter vector. However, this is known to be computationally challenging: The underlying non-parametric problem might be NP-hard, and, in addition, optimal solution sets might have exponential cardinality. Parametric approximation aims at providing polynomial-time algorithms that overcome these challenges. Instead of computing an optimal solution set, the goal is to compute an approximation set that contains only an approximate solution for every fixed parameter vector. Several new parametric approximation algorithms have been developed in recent literature. However, all of these share a common set of assumptions, which limits the class of parametric optimization problems that can be approximated. Namely, they do not allow negative parameter dependencies and have their parameter sets fixed to the positive orthant. We present a new adaptive approximation (and, also, exact) algorithm that can be applied to a wider class of linear-multi-parametric optimization problems. Our algorithm builds upon existing algorithms from both the fields of parametric and multi-objective optimization and generalizes these algorithms. In addition, we provide structural results for the transformation of parameter sets, and demonstrate that, for linear-multi-parametric maximization problems, the assumption of non-negative optimal objective values over the whole parameter set is not sufficient to ensure approximability.

</details>


### [142] [Two-Echelon Delivery Vehicle Sharing and Repositioning in Hyperconnected Urban Logistic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17608)
*Xiaoyue Liu,Benoit Montreuil*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对超互联城市物流系统，提出了一个双层车辆共享与重定位问题，利用物理互联网（PI）启用的三级物流枢纽网络和标准化集装箱配送模式，构建了多周期整数规划模型，并设计了基于分解的启发式算法。以亚特兰大都市区为案例，结果表明该超互联配送系统相比传统单层方案可减少45.0%的CO2-eq排放和16.8%的总成本，车辆重定位进一步降低成本达17.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日益增长的可持续和高效城市配送需求，以及传统物流系统效率低下、排放高的问题，促使本研究探索基于物理互联网理念的超互联城市物流解决方案。

Method: 构建了包含网关枢纽、本地枢纽和接入枢纽的三级物流枢纽网络，采用标准化集装箱配送模式实现车辆快速换箱；将问题建模为多周期整数规划，集成基于路径的服务车辆规划和基于弧的集装箱路径规划；提出基于分解的启发式算法，通过容量感知的流分配将问题按层级和区域分解为子问题。

Result: 以亚特兰大都市区为案例，超互联配送系统相比传统单层方案减少45.0%的CO2-eq排放和16.8%的总成本；车辆重定位进一步使成本降低高达17.7%。

Conclusion: 所提出的双层车辆共享与重定位模型及求解方法能有效提升城市物流系统的可持续性和经济性，物理互联网理念下的资源共享和车辆重定位机制具有显著优势。

Abstract: In response to the growing demand for sustainable and efficient urban deliveries, this study introduces a two-echelon vehicle sharing and repositioning problem for containerized delivery operations within a hyperconnected urban logistics system. We leverage a Physical Internet (PI)-enabled three-tier logistic hub network, comprising gateway, local, and access hubs, to facilitate efficient flows. By adopting containerized delivery, vehicles can rapidly swap standardized modular containers at hubs to reduce handling time. Moreover, inspired by the PI concept of open resource sharing, we determine optimal service routes within a two-echelon structure that jointly utilizes heterogeneous vehicle fleets and enables dynamic vehicle relocation across hubs. We formulate this problem as a multi-period integer program that integrates path-based service vehicle planning with arc-based container routing. To address real-world large-scale instances, we propose a decomposition-based heuristic with capacity-aware flow assignment, which partitions the problem into subproblems structured by echelons and regions. A case study on the Atlanta metropolitan area demonstrates the effectiveness of the proposed model and solution approach. Experimental results show that the two-echelon hyperconnected delivery system reduces CO2-eq emissions by 45.0% and total costs by 16.8% at full market share compared to a traditional single-echelon alternative, while enabling vehicle repositioning further lowers costs by up to 17.7%.

</details>


### [143] [A Projection-Free Algorithm for Variational Inequalities in Hilbert Spaces with Strong Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17640)
*Reinier Diaz Millan*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种在希尔伯特空间中求解点对集极大单调算子变分不等式的新方法，该方法使用半空间投影替代度量投影到凸约束集C，通过次梯度割平面和Slater修正处理可行性，结合Minty型分离半空间和Haugazeau半空间实现最佳逼近强收敛，并给出显式/近似/有限候选三种预言机实现及O(N^{-1/2})残差估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统变分不等式求解方法通常需要度量投影到可行集C上，当C由非光滑凸不等式定义时该投影计算困难；此外，点对集极大单调算子的处理在现有文献中较少见。本文旨在设计一种仅需半空间投影的算法，避免直接计算度量投影，同时处理非光滑约束和点对集算子。

Method: 1) 约束处理：使用次梯度割平面构造包含C的半空间，当试验点不可行时基于Slater点进行修正；2) 变分不等式表示：在可行图点处构造Minty型分离半空间来表征解集；3) 投影聚合：加入Haugazeau半空间以确保最佳逼近收敛性质；4) 预言机实现：提供显式、近似和有限候选三种实现方式。

Result: 1) 在Slater修正可行分离条件下，整序列强收敛到初始点在解集上的投影；2) 建立了步残差、可行性违逆和Minty间隙的O(N^{-1/2})最佳迭代残差估计；3) 记录了强单调性情形下的推论；4) 在多个大规模非光滑问题上进行了数值验证，包括二次函数极大值、离散最优控制、混合范数稀疏恢复、Cournot-Nash容量均衡及ℓ₁次微分真实点对集例子。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效克服了非光滑约束下度量投影计算困难的瓶颈，将强收敛分析和复杂度估计推广到点对集极大单调算子的通用框架，数值实验证明了算法在大规模非光滑问题上的实用性和竞争力。

Abstract: We study variational inequalities governed by a point-to-set maximal monotone operator in a real Hilbert space and constrained by a convex inequality \(C=\{x\in\Hi:c(x)\le0\}\), where the defining function \(c\) is continuous and not necessarily differentiable. The proposed method uses only projections onto intersections of half-spaces and avoids the metric projection onto \(C\). Feasibility is handled by subgradient cuts and, when a trial operator point is infeasible, by a Slater correction based on a fixed strictly feasible point. The variational inequality is represented by Minty-type separating half-spaces generated at feasible graph points of the operator, and a Haugazeau half-space is added to obtain best-approximation convergence. Under a Slater-corrected feasible-separation condition, together with explicit exact, approximate and finite-candidate oracle realisations, the whole sequence converges strongly to \(P_{S^*}(x^0)\), the projection of the initial point onto the solution set. We also derive best-iterate \(O(N^{-1/2})\) residual estimates for the step residual, feasibility violation and Minty gap. The analysis is stated directly for point-to-set maximal monotone operators, while the concrete oracle realisations include finite-dimensional single-valued models. We record the consequences of strong monotonicity in the point-to-set setting and provide numerical comparisons on nonsmooth and large-scale constraints, including maxima of convex quadratics, a discretised optimal-control problem, mixed-norm sparse recovery, a Cournot--Nash capacity equilibrium, and a genuine point-to-set \(\ell_1\)-subdifferential example.

</details>


### [144] [Robust Bayesian Portfolio Optimization with Discrepancy-based Posterior Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17643)
*Zongxia Liang,Yang Liu,Xingjian Ma*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了漂移不确定下的连续时间鲁棒贝叶斯投资组合优化问题，通过引入基于Wasserstein和L^p距离的反馈型模糊性框架解决时间不一致性，并推导了修正的HJBI方程与半显式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资产漂移不确定的情况下，投资者面临模型不确定性（模糊性）以及学习过程中的时间不一致性问题，现有方法难以在贝叶斯学习框架下同时处理这两类挑战。

Method: 采用贝叶斯滤波学习未知资产漂移，利用基于Wasserstein和L^p距离的差异度量构建模糊性集，引入反馈型模糊性框架以条件于可观测状态重新定义模糊性，导出修正的HJBI方程。对指数效用情形，将问题简化为半线性抛物型PDE并通过验证定理建立经典解的存在性。

Result: 提出了反馈型模糊性框架有效解决了时间不一致性问题，得到了修正的HJBI方程；在指数效用下获得了半显式解，并通过验证定理证明了经典解的存在性。

Conclusion: 所提出的反馈型模糊性框架能够统一处理贝叶斯学习下的漂移不确定性与模糊性，为连续时间鲁棒投资组合优化提供了理论支撑与可求解的范例。

Abstract: We study a continuous-time robust Bayesian portfolio optimization problem under drift uncertainty of risky assets. The investor learns unknown asset drifts through Bayesian filtering while considering uncertainty around posterior estimates via discrepancy-based ambiguity sets, including Wasserstein and $L^p$ distances. To address the resulting time inconsistency, we introduce a feedback-type ambiguity framework that reformulates ambiguity conditionally on observable states. This leads to a modified Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman--Isaacs (HJBI) equation characterizing the value function and the optimal strategy. For a semi-explicit solution example, we use the exponential utility to derive a reduced semilinear parabolic PDE and establish existence of classical solutions via a verification theorem.

</details>


### [145] [Gray-Box Optimization using Optimism in the Face of Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17726)
*Katrin Baumgärtner,Léo Simpson,Moritz Diehl*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究序贯灰盒优化问题，目标函数为损失函数与参数化模型的复合形式，其中模型参数未知且需从含噪声观测中迭代估计。提出一种利用已知问题结构（损失函数与参数先验集）的乐观方法，通过最小化真实目标函数的置信下界来权衡探索与利用。给出了详细的遗憾分析，由于采用了多输出线性最小二乘参数置信集的最新界，改进了一类线性随机bandit的特例结果。数值实验证明了结构利用方法的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒优化方法（如随机线性bandit）未能充分利用问题中已知的结构信息（损失函数与参数先验集），导致样本效率不足。本文旨在提出一种能够显式利用这些结构信息的序贯优化方法，以提升优化性能。

Method: 提出一种基于乐观面对不确定性原则的结构利用方法，通过最小化真实目标函数的置信下界来实现探索与利用的权衡。该方法利用了损失函数形式和参数先验集合等已知结构，并采用多输出线性最小二乘估计构建参数置信集，结合最新的置信集界进行遗憾分析。

Result: 在理论方面，给出了所提方法的详细遗憾分析，由于使用了多输出线性最小二乘参数置信集的最新界，在特例（线性随机bandit）上改进了现有最优结果。数值实验表明，结构利用方法显著优于结构无关的方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的灰盒优化方法通过利用已知问题结构（损失函数和参数先验）能够有效提升序贯优化性能，在理论上改进了线性bandit的遗憾界，在数值上验证了结构利用的优势。

Abstract: This paper considers sequential gray-box optimization where the objective function is given as the composition of a loss function and a parametric model. Crucially, the parameters of the model are unknown and need to be iteratively estimated from noisy observations of the model outputs. This problem setup generalizes the parametric black-box optimization problem known as (contextual) stochastic linear bandit. To address the sequential gray-box optimization problem, we propose a structure-exploiting method that leverages known problem structure given in terms of the loss function and an a priori set of admissible parameters. The method is based on the principle of optimism in the face of uncertainty and trades off exploration and exploitation by minimizing a lower confidence bound on the true objective function. We provide a detailed regret analysis of the novel method, improving on state-of-the-art results for the special case of linear stochastic bandits due to the use of a recently published bound for the parameter confidence sets arising in multi-output linear least-squares estimation. Numerical examples illustrate the superior performance of structure-exploiting methods compared to structure-agnostic approaches.

</details>


### [146] [Symplectic Transversality and Endpoint Green Estimates for Finite-Horizon Pontryagin Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17762)
*Pyuyi Chufeng Huang,Zikang Song,Xingshu Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限时域离散时间Pontryagin边值系统在光滑控制消去后的水平一致局部分支，通过两点端点逆、稳定-不稳定边界横截性、端点修正Green估计和加权压缩映射，证明了存在性、唯一性、Lipschitz依赖性和一阶展开式，且常数与水平无关。框架覆盖光滑非线性端点映射，辛和Riccati准则可验证逆假设，数值实验展示了证书和水平一致一阶展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决有限时域离散时间Pontryagin边值系统中局部分支的存在性、唯一性及其对时域水平的一致依赖性，为非线性最优控制问题提供严格的数学基础。

Method: 利用两点端点逆（从稳定-不稳定边界横截性验证）、端点修正Green估计、加权压缩映射技术，结合辛和Riccati准则验证逆假设。

Result: 建立了水平一致的存在性、唯一性、Lipschitz依赖性和一阶展开式；覆盖了可镇定线性二次系统（含非交换耦合数据）；数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 提供了一套系统的分析框架，确保有限时域Pontryagin边值问题的解在时域水平变化时具有一致的良好性质，拓展了经典结果的适用范围。

Abstract: We study horizon-uniform local branches of finite-horizon discrete-time Pontryagin boundary value systems after smooth control elimination. The central input is a two-point endpoint inverse for the linearization. We verify this inverse from scaled stable--unstable boundary transversality, prove the associated endpoint-corrected Green estimate, and combine it with weighted contractions to obtain existence, uniqueness, Lipschitz dependence, and first-order expansions with constants independent of the horizon. The framework covers smooth nonlinear endpoint maps, including the original Pontryagin rows that fix the initial state and couple the terminal costate to the terminal state. Symplectic and Riccati criteria verify the inverse hypothesis at the level of the matrix data; in particular, every stabilizable linear-quadratic system with invertible dynamics and definite weights is covered, including noncommuting coupled data. A numerical section illustrates the certificates and the horizon-uniform first-order expansion.

</details>


### [147] [Operator Learning for PDE Backstepping Control of Parabolic Equations on Time-Varying Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17766)
*Jinrun Yan,Kai Liu,Zhong-Jie Han*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于学习的边界控制框架，利用神经算子（DeepONet）近似时变反推设计算子，实现抛物型方程在时变空间域上的实时稳定控制，加速比接近三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDE反推方法虽为时变空间域系统提供系统化理论框架，但其实时实现受限于需在演化域上反复求解时变核PDE，计算代价高昂。

Method: 将时变反推设计形式化为从运动边界轨迹到反推核的算子映射；通过将核方程映射到固定参考域，证明核关于边界轨迹的连续依赖性，为神经算子逼近提供理论依据；采用DeepONet离线学习核算子，在线生成时变核而无需重复求解核PDE。

Result: 闭环系统在任意有限时间区间上具有指数衰减估计；数值基准显示，所提方法避免了重复在线求解时变核PDE，与传统数值求解器相比加速近三个数量级，实现了时变空间域系统的实时稳定。

Conclusion: 所提出的神经算子框架有效克服了时变反推控制实时实现的瓶颈，为移动边界PDE系统的在线控制提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper develops a learning-based boundary control framework for stabilizing a parabolic equation defined on time-varying spatial domain. Although the partial differential equation (PDE) backstepping method provides a systematic theoretical framework for such moving-boundary systems, its real-time implementation is hindered by the need to repeatedly solve time-varying kernel PDEs on evolving domains. To overcome this limitation, we first formulate the time-varying backstepping design as an operator that maps the moving-boundary trajectory to the corresponding backstepping kernel. By mapping the time-varying domain of the backstepping kernel equation onto a fixed reference domain, we establish the continuous dependence of the kernel on the moving-boundary trajectory, which provides the theoretical basis for approximating the backstepping design operator by a neural operator. Based on the approximate kernel operator, we construct the corresponding boundary feedback controller to stabilize the system. It is shown that the closed-loop system admits an exponential decay estimate on any prescribed finite time interval. For numerical implementation, DeepONet is employed to learn the time-varying kernel operator from offline-generated numerical kernel solutions and is subsequently deployed online to generate the required time-varying kernels without repeatedly solving the kernel PDE. Numerical benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed neural-operator-based implementation bypasses repeated online solution of the time-varying kernel PDE, achieves a significant acceleration of close to three orders of magnitude compared with conventional numerical kernel solvers, and thus enables real-time stabilization of the system on time-varying spatial domain.

</details>


### [148] [Description of the set of admissible piecewise linear routes with n turns in the three dimensional case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17768)
*Nefedov V. N*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了三维空间中连接两点A、B的等长分段线性多边形链，在每段长度相等且每个内点转角绝对值有界（φ∈(0,π)）的条件下，给出了所有内顶点可达集合的精确描述（定理1），并证明了该集合中任意点均可构造出满足参数的多边形链（引理1）。进而得到了所有角点序列的显式公式，为枚举算法和考虑路径代价与转弯代价的优化问题奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人路径规划、离散几何等领域中，需要描述满足转角约束的多边形链所有内顶点的可达范围，以支持后续的枚举与优化算法设计。

Method: 通过几何分析，在条件nφ≤π下刻画了所有内顶点所属集合，并利用引理1证明该集合中任意点均可实现；基于此推导出角点序列的显式公式。

Result: 得到了内顶点可达集合的完整描述（定理1），以及角点序列的显式公式。

Conclusion: 该描述可为枚举满足条件的多边形链和求解含路径代价与转弯代价的优化问题提供理论基础。

Abstract: We consider piecewise linear polygonal chains connecting two given points $A, B \in \mathbb{R}^3$ and consisting of exactly $n+1$ segments (i.e., having $n$ turning points). The absolute value of the turning angle at each interior point is bounded by a given number $\varphi \in (0,π)$. Under the condition $n\varphi \leq π$, we describe the set to which all interior vertices of such a polygonal chain belong (Theorem 1). It is proved that for any point $B^{(1)}$ from this set, there exists a polygonal chain with the specified parameters (Lemma 1). Based on these results, we obtain an explicit formula describing the set of all admissible sequences $(B^{(1)}, \ldots, B^{(n)})$ of the angular points of the polygonal chain. The obtained description can serve as a basis for constructing algorithms to enumerate admissible polygonal chains and to solve optimization problems for an objective function that accounts for the cost of traversing the segments and the cost of turns.

</details>


### [149] [A Bilevel Optimization Model for Bottom-Up Coordination of Multiple Low-Voltage Energy Communities and the Medium-Voltage Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17769)
*Fernando García-Muñoz,Sebastián Dávila,Luis Rojo-González,Cristian Duran-Mateluna*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Stackelberg博弈的上下级协调框架，其中低压社区聚合商作为领导者决定DER运行和边界能量交换，中压网络作为跟随者进行经济调度。通过KKT条件实现精确单层重构，并开发了LDD-ADMM分布式协调算法保护数据隐私。在IEEE 33节点和六个欧洲206节点系统上验证，LDD-ADMM与精确重构的平均相对偏差为1.7e-4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式能源（DER）渗透率不断提高，低压网络转变为含能量社区的主动系统，其发电、储能和能量交换活动需要与上游中压网络增强协调，现有方法缺乏高效的上下级协调机制。

Method: 采用Stackelberg博弈结构，LV社区聚合商作为领导者，MV网络作为跟随者；利用KKT条件将双层模型精确重构为单层凸优化；结合LDD与ADMM开发分布式协调算法实现并行协调与数据隐私保护。

Result: LDD-ADMM算法与精确单层重构的平均相对偏差为1.7e-4，偏差仅在廉价资源稀缺时段出现；领导者的决策可能导致跟随者成本高于其独立最优调度；与可行性单层松弛相比，所提方法能实现更优的MV资源配置。

Conclusion: 提出的底层协调方案能有效实现LV与MV网络的协调运行，分布式算法在保护隐私的同时保持了高精度，但领导者决策可能增加跟随者成本，需进一步考虑公平性机制。

Abstract: The increasing penetration of distributed energy resources (DERs) is transforming low-voltage (LV) networks into active systems, including energy communities, whose generation, storage, and energy exchange activities require enhanced coordination with upstream medium-voltage (MV) networks. In the proposed Stackelberg structure, a local market operator aggregates multiple LV communities and acts as a single leader, determining DER operations and boundary energy exchanges, while the MV network serves as the follower, ensuring efficient system feasibility through an economic dispatch that includes both conventional and utility-scale PV generation. The proposed bottom-up coordination scheme concentrates discrete DER scheduling at the LV level while the MV level retains a convex continuous formulation, enabling an exact single-level reformulation via the Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT) conditions. In addition, a distributed coordination algorithm that combines Lagrangian Dual Decomposition (LDD) with the Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM) is developed to coordinate LV communities in parallel while preserving data confidentiality. The framework is validated using the IEEE 33-bus system at the MV level and six European 206-bus LV test feeders. Results indicate that the LDD-ADMM algorithm closely matches the exact reformulation, with an average relative deviation of 1.7e-4, with deviations confined to periods of scarcity for the cheap resource. Furthermore, leaders' decisions can induce operating conditions that increase followers' costs relative to their independently optimal dispatch, a pattern reinforced by comparison with a feasibility-based single-level relaxation that satisfies the required energy exchanges but fails to achieve a cost-efficient allocation of MV resources.

</details>


### [150] [Optimizing the Principal Coefficient of Elliptic Equations using $L^p$-regularity, $p < \infty$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17788)
*Ala' Alalabi,Lorena Bociu,Paul Manns*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究带全变分正则化和控制约束的椭圆偏微分方程系数识别问题，通过利用$W^{1,s}$正则性建立控制-状态算子关于$L^q$范数的可微性，推导了缩减目标泛函的一阶和二阶可微性结果，并分析了基于$L^r$信赖域的非光滑信赖域方法的收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献依赖$L^\infty$范数下的连续性和可微性，这虽能推导最优性条件，但忽视了扰动的空间范围，导致与$L^q$（$q<\infty$）范数存在定性差异，不适用于数值算法。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 利用$W^{1,s}$正则性结果建立控制-状态算子关于$L^q$范数的可微性；基于此推导缩减目标泛函的一阶和二阶可微性；通过受限次微分刻画全变分半范数并给出乘子正则性；提出基于$L^r$信赖域（$r>0.5q$）的非光滑信赖域方法并证明其收敛到一阶稳定点。

Result: 建立了控制-状态算子关于$L^q$范数的可微性；获得了缩减目标泛函的一阶和二阶可微性结果；给出了带全变分正则化的一阶最优性条件；证明了非光滑信赖域方法的收敛性。

Conclusion: 本文克服了$L^\infty$框架的局限性，为带全变分正则化和控制约束的椭圆PDE系数识别问题提供了更适用于数值算法的理论框架，并证明了相应信赖域方法的收敛性。

Abstract: We study coefficient identification problems for elliptic partial differential equations with total variation regularization and control constraints. Existing related literature relies on continuity and differentiability properties of the control-to-state operator with respect to the $L^\infty$-norm. While this is sufficient for deriving optimality conditions, it is not well-suited for numerical algorithms, as it neglects the spatial extent of perturbations and leads to a qualitative discrepancy compared to $L^q$-norms with $q < \infty$. In this work, we address this gap by exploiting $W^{1,s}$-regularity results to establish differentiability properties of the control-to-state operator with respect to $L^q$-norms for finite $q$. Based on this framework, we derive first- and second-order differentiability results for the reduced objective functional and establish first-order optimality conditions involving a restricted subdifferential characterization of the total variation seminorm and corresponding regularity of the associated multipliers. Building on this, we analyze a nonsmooth trust-region method based on an $L^r$-trust region for $r > 0.5 q$ and prove its convergence to first-order stationary points.

</details>


### [151] [Koopman Lifting with Certified Error Bounds for Joint Inference in Nonlinear Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17797)
*Chuansen Peng,Xiaojing Shen,Yunmin Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出Koopman-GKFA框架，通过Koopman算子嵌入将非线性网络动力学提升为近似线性系统，结合群稀疏卡尔曼滤波与ADMM优化，实现非线性图动力系统中隐节点状态与未知网络拓扑的联合推理，在理论上给出同态引理、线性收敛保证和三误差界分解，实验优于EKF/UKF/粒子滤波方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性图动力系统中，连续节点状态与离散网络拓扑相互纠缠，任一量的精确恢复都严重依赖于另一方，现有方法难以同时有效求解这一联合推理问题。

Method: 利用Koopman算子嵌入（可分离节点字典）将非线性动力学提升为近似线性系统，在此基础上设计群稀疏卡尔曼滤波（GKFA）进行状态估计，并采用带指数遗忘因子的群稀疏ADMM进行拓扑推理，三者构成统一框架。

Result: 在Kuramoto振子、Hill动力学基因调控网络合成基准以及NGSIM US-101、DREAM4真实数据集上，Koopman-GKFA在状态估计和拓扑恢复两方面均一致优于EKF、UKF和粒子滤波联合估计器，且具有多项式级计算复杂度和高维鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过Koopman算子线性化与群稀疏优化的有机结合，为非线性网络结构-状态联合推理提供了理论保证与高效算法，三误差分解为后续改进指明了方向。

Abstract: Jointly inferring latent node states and unknown network topology in nonlinear graphical dynamical systems is a fundamental yet largely unsolved problem, where the mutual entanglement of continuous states and discrete structure renders accurate recovery of either quantity critically dependent on the other. We propose \textbf{Koopman-GKFA} (Koopman Group-sparse Kalman Filter--ADMM), a unified framework that lifts nonlinear network dynamics into an approximately linear system via Koopman operator embedding with a separable node-wise dictionary, enabling optimal linear filtering for state estimation and provably convergent convex optimization for topology inference. Three theoretical contributions underpin the framework: (i)~a \emph{structural homomorphism lemma} proving that, under a separable-dictionary condition, block sparsity of the lifted coupling operator is isomorphic to the graph topology, providing the rigorous foundation for group-sparse regularization; (ii)~a block-structured group-sparse ADMM topology subproblem with certified linear convergence, extended by an exponential forgetting factor to track time-varying topologies; and (iii)~a \emph{three-term certified mean-squared error bound} that decomposes total estimation error into Koopman truncation, observation noise, and topology residual components, with monotone consistency established as the dictionary dimension grows. Extensive experiments on synthetic benchmarks (Kuramoto oscillators, Hill-kinetics gene-regulatory networks) and real-world datasets (NGSIM US-101, DREAM4) demonstrate that Koopman-GKFA consistently outperforms EKF-, UKF-, and particle-filter-based joint estimators in both state estimation and topology recovery, while exhibiting polynomial computational scaling and strong robustness in high-dimensional nonlinear settings.

</details>


### [152] [Kalman structure and observability for transport systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17814)
*Yacouba Simpore*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究常系数一阶双曲系统在实直线上的可观性与可控性，发现即使满足Kalman秩条件，标准的L^2可观测性估计可能因部分状态仅通过动力学被检测而失效。Kalman结构决定了合适的可观测性估计：经k步代数步骤后变得可观测的分量，在低频傅里叶域中需乘以|ξ|^{2k}权重。由此建立Kalman自适应观测空间，并进一步证明对角化系统在有界间隙观测集上的局部可观测性，以及将构造推广至实谱含Jordan块的系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典可观测性理论中Kalman秩条件是充分必要条件，但在双曲型PDE系统中，部分状态变量仅通过系统动力学间接被观测，导致标准L^2范数估计失效。需要重新理解Kalman结构如何影响观测能力，并建立适合的观测空间与估计框架。

Method: 采用傅里叶分析方法，通过考察观测映射的代数结构（Kalman矩阵的秩结构）来刻画各分量在傅里叶域中的可检测性；引入加权范数（权重|ξ|^{2k}）量化不同分量的可观测程度；对有界间隙观测集和对角化/非对角化系统分别进行理论分析。

Result: Kalman结构决定了各分量在傅里叶域中需要额外乘以|ξ|^{2k}权重才能获得可观测性估计；构造了Kalman自适应观测空间，使得该空间上的标准L^2估计成立；对角化系统在有界间隙观测集上仍可局部可观测；整个构造可推广至实谱含Jordan块的系统。

Conclusion: 对于常系数一阶双曲系统，Kalman秩条件不足以保证标准L^2可观测性估计，必须根据Kalman结构调整观测空间的赋权方式。该工作为部分观测下的双曲系统控制理论提供了更精确的数学框架。

Abstract: We study observability and controllability for constant-coefficient first-order hyperbolic systems on the real line when only part of the state is observed or controlled. Even when the Kalman rank condition holds, the usual \(L^2(\mathbb R)^N\)-observability estimate may fail because some components are detected only through the dynamics. We show that the Kalman structure determines the appropriate observability estimate. A component that becomes visible after \(k\) algebraic steps is measured at low Fourier frequencies with a weight of order \(|ξ|^{2k}\) in the Fourier variable. This yields a natural Kalman-adapted observation space for the system. We also prove localized observability for diagonalizable systems on observation sets with uniformly bounded gaps and, separately, extend the whole-line construction to systems with real spectrum and Jordan blocks.

</details>


### [153] [PepsiCo Deploys AI-Driven Pricing and Promotion Optimization at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17941)
*Aleix Llenas,Eduardo Salazar-Treviño,Francisco Leskovar,Francesc Pons-Llopis,Federico Todeschini,Deepika Gowda,David Bofill,Laxmi Anish,Michael Cleavinger*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了百事公司开发并部署的两个大规模优化系统——PromoAI和PricingAI，分别用于促销日历优化和基础定价优化，将机器学习预测与数学规划相结合，实现了企业级自动化收入增长管理决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高度竞争和动态的市场中，企业需要同时考虑需求弹性、竞争对手行动、渠道和市场约束以及财务目标来进行定价与促销规划。随着问题维度和相互依赖性的增加，人工或传统方法变得次优且不足，因此需要基于运筹学的可扩展数据驱动决策支持系统。

Method: PromoAI将基于机器学习的促销预测与混合整数线性规划模型相结合，优化跨贸易渠道的促销日历，搜索数百万种产品-促销-时机组合。PricingAI使用贝叶斯层次模型估计自身和交叉价格弹性及竞争交互作用，再将其输入非线性规划引擎，在运营约束下推荐符合收入和利润目标的定价变更。

Result: 两个系统在百事公司成功部署，证明了先进优化在大规模企业环境中的可行性和可扩展性，实现了数据驱动且与战略业务目标一致的企业级自动化决策。

Conclusion: 将统计学习与数学规划相结合，能够为大规模企业环境中的收入增长管理提供可行且可扩展的优化解决方案，支持自动化决策制定。

Abstract: Effective pricing and promotion planning constitutes a central pillar of strategic revenue management for firms operating in highly competitive and dynamic markets. These planning activities require the simultaneous consideration of demand elasticity, competitor actions, channel and market specific constraints, and financial objectives. As the dimensionality and interdependencies inherent in these problems increase, manual or traditional approaches become suboptimal and insufficient. In this context, Operations Research provides a robust methodological foundation for scalable, data-driven decision support systems that can optimize complex planning processes across large product and customer portfolios.
  This paper presents two large-scale optimization systems developed and deployed at PepsiCo to support Revenue Growth Management initiatives: PromoAI and PricingAI. PromoAI couples machine learning-based promotional forecasts with a mixed-integer linear programming model to optimize promotional calendars across trade channels, searching millions of product-promotion-timing combinations for the one that maximizes PepsiCo and retailer revenues subject to customizable business constraints. PricingAI optimizes base prices across product portfolios over multi-period horizons, using Bayesian hierarchical models to estimate own- and cross-price elasticities and competitive interactions, then feeding these into a nonlinear programming engine that recommends price changes aligned with revenue and margin targets under operational constraints.
  Together, these systems demonstrate the feasibility and scalability of advanced optimization in large-scale enterprise environments. They highlight the value of integrating statistical learning with mathematical programming to enable enterprise-level, automated decision-making that is both data-informed and aligned with strategic business objectives.

</details>
